Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The geometric operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 440. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 952 with 2 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 440 + O(1/n^952). Summing over all modes and applying the geometric convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
